mukhabandhaḥ |
--
*
smṛtinibandhakāreṣu kila hemādriḥ prāyeṇa savrvopari varvvartti |
1
sa khalu caitrapāladevatanayasya devagiripradeśādhipateḥ mahārāja-
cakravarttino mahādevasya pradhānāmātyapadamalañcakāra, ayañca mahā-
deva: praśītyadhikaikādaśaśatatame śakābde devagirirājasiṃhāsana-
madhiruroha iti pratnatattvavidāṃ vyāhāraḥ |
tena khalu hemādriṇa viracitasya subrahata caturvvarga cintāmaṇi-
nāmnaḥ smṛtinibandhasya prāmāṇyaṃ sarvairevā'rvvāgbhavai raghunandanabhaṭṭā-
cāryyaprabhṛtibhi: smṛtinibandhakārairavisaṃvādamaṅgīkṛtam | granya-
gauraveṇa vyavasthāpanīyāvaśyaka viṣayātibāhulyena asandigdhaprāmā-
khyānāṃ śrutismṛtipurāṇetihāsādibhyaḥ samuddhṛtānāṃ vacanānāṃ
prācuryeṇa ca ayaṃ caturvvarga cintāmaṇiḥ sarvvathaivā'nvarthanāmā iti
sarveṣāṃ smṛtinibandharasikānāṃ spapannaṃ nirvvacanam |
vividhaprācya-pratīcyāvaśyaka vidyāsamudayapracāronnatisampādana-
samuditadimaṇḍalo maṇḍanāyamānayaśojālasya
paramasammānabhāja:
supratiṣṭhitasya 'āsiyāṭik sosāiṭī' iti viśvaviśrutanānā
prasiḍasya viddasamājasya ādeśaṃ samadhigamya tasyaiva caturvvarga-
cintāmaṇeḥ prāyaścittakhaṇḍākhyaṃ bhāgamekamimaṃ granyaṃ mudrayitumaṅgī-
[[page 10-to-10]]
( 100 )
kṛto mayā mahatā''dareṇa mahān khalu prayāmaḥ |
daśa varṣāṇi
yāvat sampādyate'sya granthasya mayā mudraṇavyāpāraḥ | sa cā'yaṃ
sāmprataṃ samāptimupagataḥ |
asya granthasya mudraṇasampādanāya trīṇi tāvat hastalikhi-
lāni pustakāni mayā adhigatāni yathāmati paryāloci-
tāni ca | tatraikaṃ āsiyāṭiksosāiṭīkarttṛpakṣeḥ kāśota:
krītaṃ, dvitīyaṃ kāśīta eva bālamukundamālavimahodayasya karuṇyā
mayā adhigataṃ tṛtīyaṃ vārāṇasītaeva labdhamekamādarśapustaka -
supajīvya āsiyāṭiksosāiṭī karttṛpateḥ lekhitam | teṣu
eteṣu triṣu pustakeṣu mālavimahodayakkṛpayā samadhigataṃ pustakaṃ
vihāya pustakaddayam aśuddhibahulam antarā'ntarā ca nitarāma-
saṃlagnapāṭhañca |
āsiyāṭika sosāiṭīnāmnā prasihavidda samāja prakāśitebhyaḥ
caturkhargacintāmaṇerbhāgāntaragranyebhyaḥ sarvvathā vilakṣaṇatvena tāvat
pratibhāti śrayaṃ prāyaścittakhaṇḍātmako granthabhāgaḥ |
tadetadvailakṣaṇyamodṛk suspaṣṭam yadetatparyyālocanena caturvvarga-
cintāmaṇikartṛtayā suprasiddhasya nikhiladhammaśāstrārtha pārāvāra-
pārīṇasya śrīmato hemādreḥ katigvājyaṃ prāyaścittakhaṇḍātmako
bhāgo na veti mahān khalu saṃśayo me manasi uttarottaramupacaya-
mabhyupagacchanneva varīvattate |
tathāhi sarvveṣveva prāmāṇika smṛtinibandheṣu yeṣu pāpaviśeṣeṣu
yāni prāyaścittāni vairvacanervyavasthāpitāni sāmprataṃ samupa-
[[page 11-to-11]]
( 10 )
labhyante, asmin khalu prāyavittakhaṇḍe teṣveva pāpeṣu prāyeṇa
taddiruddāni prāyaścittāni taddilakṣaṇaireva vacanairvyavasthāpitāni
dṛśyante |
anyacca brahmahatyāsurāpānagurvvaṅganāgamanatatsaṃsargākhyeṣu mahā-
pātakeṣu prāyaścittapratipādakatvena yāni saṃhitāpurāṇavacanāni
sarvvereva itara nibandhakārerekamatyena uddhṛtāni, prāyeṇa tāni
sarvvāṇyeva vacanāni asmin granthe nitarāmupekṣitāni iti ca
mahaduvelakṣaṇyam |
caturvvarga cintāmaṇeḥ etadugranyavyatiriktācāra saṃskāradānādi-
pratipādakagranyabhāgeṣu tu naitādṛk tattadarthapratipādaketara-
smṛtinibandhebhyo vailakṣaṇyaṃ prāyeṇa dṛśyate ; tathāhi caturvvarga-
cintāmaṇerācārādipratipādaka tattat khaṇḍātmakabhāgaviśeṣu yairvacanai-
yeṃ tāvadācārādayaḥ yathā ca pratipāditā, itaranibandheṣu
api satyapi kvacit kvacidoṣadvailakṣaṇye prāyeṇa tairvacanaiste
evā''cārādayaḥ tathaiva ca pratipāditā iti nipuṇataramupa-
lakṣitam |
T
aparañca etasmin prāyaścittakhaṇḍe manvādimaharṣipraṇītāsu
dharmasaṃhitāsu sthitāni iti kṛtvā yāni vacanāni samuddhṛtāni,
mahadāścaryametad yat - sāmprataṃ mudriteṣu hastalikhiteṣu vā manvādi-
maharṣipraṇītasaṃhitāgrantheṣu tāni vacanāni prāyeṇa nopalabhyante,
sahṛdayānāṃ pratyayotpādanāya kānicit tathāvidhāni vacanāni
udāhriyante yathā -
[[page 12-to-12]]
( 11° )
asya granthasya 13 pṛṣṭhe brahmahatyāprakaraṇe-
“ddiśatenāpagurdadyāt sahasreṇa hataddijaḥ |
dviśatena tadā daṇḍayā vṛḍā brajaṃ nṛpaḥ || iti
nitarāmaspaṣṭārthakaṃ vacanamidaṃ mānavīyamiti kṛtvā samuddhṛtaṃ,
sāmprataṃ mudriteṣu hastalikhiteṣu vā manusaṃhitāpustakeṣu yathāvidhi
anusandadhatā mayā nā'yaṃ ślokaḥ samupalabdha iti mahadetadasya
granyasya prāmāṇyasaṃśayakāraṇam |
tathā asya granthasya 586 pṛṣṭhe-
-
śṛṇu dhammaṃja vakṣyāmi svarṇakāmadughāṃ sakṛt |
yoddijaḥ pratigṭahṇāti sa sadyaḥ patitobhavet ||
tasyaiva niṣkṛtirbhūpa punarbrahmopadeśataḥ |
aṣṭalakṣajapādrājan vyayaṃ vā'ṣṭamabhāgataḥ ||
abhiṣekeṇa vā śambhīryajñairvā, sarvvadaciṇaiḥ 1
etaiḥ śuddhimavāpnoti ubhayorlokayorhitam || iti
ete ttrayaḥ ślokā bahavaścānya evaṃ jātīyakā mahābhāratīyā
iti kṛtvā atra samuddhṛtā: - samupalabhyante, parantu sāmpratikeṣu
mudritamahābhāratapustakeṣu mahatā prayāsena anusandadhatā mayā
naiṣāmeko'pi ślokaḥ samupalabdhaḥ |
evameva kūmmaṃ purāṇamatsya purāṇaliṅgapurāṇapadmapurāṇādināmnā
yāni vacanāni granye'smin samuddhṛtāni tāni ca prāyeṇa teṣu
purāṇa pustakeṣu mudriteṣu hastalikhiteṣu vā nopalabhyante |
evamādīni asya granyasya aprāmāṇya sandehaprayojakāni rūpāṇi
[[page 13-to-13]]
( || / 0 )
11/0
itarasmṛtinibandhebhyaḥ caturvvarga cintāmaṇerbhāgāntarebhyaśca mahānti
velakṣaṇyāni ca bahuśaḥ samadhigagya śrayaṃ granthaḥ caturvvarga-
cintāmaṇikāreṇa prathitayaśasā hemādriṇā racita eva vā
navā iti mahān khalu me manasi saṃśayaḥ, sa khalvayaṃ
samīcīno vā navā iti tīkṣṇadhiya: pramāṇapārāvārapārīṇāḥ
sahṛdayāḥ śiṣṭā pava vicārayantu iti savinayaṃ sabahumānaśca teṣu
vinivedayati |
tarkabhūṣaṇopādhika-
śrīpramathanāthaśamrmā
kalikātārājakīya saṃskṛta vidyālayadhammaṃ mīmāṃsāśāstrādhyāpakaḥ |

tatsavrvvaṃ niṣphalaṃ yāti na labheta kriyāphalam ||

viddatsu dhammaḥ praṣṭavyaḥ svakam phalalipsubhiḥ ||

brahmasūtraṃ tridhā cchitvā piśitāśanavāhanam |
āvāhayitvā tadbhāle 'śūlaṃ taptamayaṃ likhet ||
(1) nareśvaraḥ iti kāśīpustake pāṭhaḥ |
(2) upagūheta iti krītapustake pāṭhaḥ |
(3) pattaṇe iti krotalekhita pustakayoḥ pāṭhaḥ |
(4) vimo'śubhāṃ giramiti kāśīpustakapāṭhaḥ I
(5) luṭitvā tacchiroruhān iti krota lekhita pustakayoḥ pāṭhaḥ |
(6) śunaṃ taptamayaṃ iti krotale khatapustakayoḥ pāṭhaḥ |

alpasuvarṇāpahāraprāyaścittamāha-
gurūṇāṃ yajñakarttṛṇāṃ dharmiṣṭhānāṃ tathaivaca |
śrotriyāṇāṃ dvijānāntu hatvā hema kathaṃ bhavet ||

punardhṛtvā' tu viprebhyaḥ prāyaścittavidhiḥ katham ||
tatra sāntapanaṃ kṛtvā dvādaśāhopavāsataḥ |
śaddhimāpnoti viprendrāanyathā patitobhavet ||
trasareṇusamaṃ hema hṛtvā kuyyāt samāhitam |
prāṇāyāmaddayaṃ samyak tena śudhyati vatsarāt ||

yabhellajjāṃ vihayā''śu sa vai narakamaśrute ||
jāvāli:-
kharīmuṣṭrauñca var̤avāṃ yabheddipromadāturaḥ |
rauravaṃ narakaṃ yāti yāvadābhūtasaṃplavam ||
tasya doṣavināśārthaṃ prāyaścittamudīritam |
khare cāndraṃ tathoṣṭuṃ ca parārka var̤avāgame ||
etena śuddhimāpnoti puna: saṃskārapūrvvakam |
pañcagavyaṃ pivetṛ-paścāt śuddhobhavati niścayaḥ || iti
kṣatriya-vaśyayorviproktāda dviguṇaṃ prāyaścittaṃ viśodhanam |
iti hemādrau kharoṣṭravar̤avāmaithunaprāyaścittam |
(1) bāheti krītapustake nāsti |

(3) yadi iti krota lekhita pustakapāṭhaḥ |
(4) vaḍavāṃ samudra' ca iti lekhita pustakapāṭhaḥ |

gautamaḥ – prajāṃ daśame varṣe svauprajāṃ dvādaśe tathā |
mṛtaprajāṃ pañcadaśe sadyastvapriyavādinīm ||
apriyavādonāma kalauyuge sākṣāduvyabhicāraḥ | yadyapi apriyāṇi
bharttṛviṣaye kalau yuge 'bahūni santi, tathāpi sācādu vyabhicāra
evāpriyavādaḥ tadā tyājyā | anyathā doṣamāha-
gautama :-
vyabhicārādṛte patnīṃ yovipraḥ saṃparityajet |
bhrūṇahatyāmavāpnoti ṛtukālavyatikramāt ||
kharājinaṃ bahirloma paridhāya ' sa yatnataḥ |
śarāvapāttramādāya bhikṣārthaṃ grāmamāviśet ||
'svadāravyatikramiṇe bhikṣāṃ dehīti yācayet |
saptāgārākhyaṭitvātha bhojayet sāyamādarāt ||
ṣaṇmāsamevaṃ kṛtvā tu śuddhimāpnoti paurvvikom |
(1) satasyeti bekhita pustakapāṭhaḥ |
(2) pūjyate iti bekhitapustakapāṭhaḥ |
(3) baṃhūni ina prājñāpitāni iti krītapustakapāṭhaḥ |
(4) krīta pustake nāsti |
(5) samannataḥ iti krīta - bekhitapustakapāṭhaḥ |
(6) dāra vyatikramayena iti lekhita pustakapāṭhaḥ |

tadāha āpastambaḥ
hamādri: |
